Player_Black (to Proxy):

I see this is a pawn race position. Let me check the legal moves more carefully - I already have them. The key insight: pushing g5-g4 would take 4 moves to promote while White's b5 pawn takes only 3 moves to promote, so White would win that race. Instead, pushing the a-pawn also takes 3 moves to promote, matching White's pace, but since I move first in each exchange, I'll promote at the same "move number" as White but before White's actual promotion move completes.

Let me push the a-pawn to start the race on equal footing where I have the tempo advantage.

make_move a4a3
